## Executive Recommendation

- Keep direct canonical owner and emitter [UID:0000FW] `VirusChecker`; keep source root [UID:0000P5] `NexusTK/security/VirusChecker.cpp`.
- Do not split this range. It is one contiguous compiler-emission family for one class member, two callbacks, one `std::set` operation family, and destructor/EH consequences. Documentation can map source-authored versus generated bodies without inventing nine source files or source methods.
- Populate UID0001NW CPP with only the three authored functions. Put all declarations in UID0000FW H. Put module-static callback/export typedefs and globals at emitter position `10`, UID0001NV method bodies at `20`, and UID0001NW method/callback bodies at `30`; put UID0000FW at file-root position `0` and use CPP `#include "VirusChecker.h"` plus `[[CHILDREN]]`.
- Use a private `ModulePathString` typedef over `mystr::StringBase<char, mystr::mychar_traits<char> >`, a private `ModulePathLess` comparator using `strcmp`, and `std::set<ModulePathString, ModulePathLess>`. This is self-contained, period-plausible, and reflects the observed byte-string lexical comparisons without exposing decompiler labels.
- The remaining no-normal-activation route limits broad runtime confidence only. It does not block exact reconstruction of retained source or justify an empty emitter.

## Inference Research Guidance Check

- Facts, documentation, and inference were separated. Exact addresses, stack cleanup, xrefs, bytes, types, and UDT offsets are IDA facts. Existing source names and routing are documentation evidence. Original identifier spellings and unused vendor parameter semantics are inference.
- Existing assumptions were challenged rather than copied: the future-child no-code policy, vendor `__stdcall` typedefs, untyped callbacks, `String` forward declaration, and the then-current pre-callback generated declaration order were rejected.
- The source reconstruction is required to look human-written and period-plausible while preserving runtime behavior. Final names therefore use `InsertModulePath`, `ModulePathString`, `ModulePathSet`, and typed callbacks rather than `sub_5C...`, `_DWORD`, `unk_69BF80`, or raw node fields.
- Stale Wave2/Wave3 mentions in older docs were ignored as proof. The accepted research used then-current docs/generated artifacts, bounded live IDA evidence, exact PE mapping, and relevant rechecked reports; this reconciliation re-read current docs/generated artifacts and retains all IDA observations only as explicitly dated history.

## Heuristic / Inference Reanalysis And Validation

- `0x005c0940`: best source inference is `VirusChecker::InsertModulePath(const char *)`. It constructs an ANSI StringBase value, obtains a lower-case copy, uniquely inserts it into the embedded ordered set, and destroys temporaries. The no-xref result is retained-code evidence, not a reason to invent a raw helper or omit source.
- `0x005c09f0`: best source inference is a private static `__stdcall` module callback. Its body is behaviorally the same as calling `InsertModulePath`; compiler inlining explains the duplicate lowered String/set sequence. Rejected alternative: a second independent path-normalization function, because it has identical class context, string sequence, set offset, and continuation return.
- `0x005c0aa0`: best source inference is a private static `__stdcall` process callback. It forwards process ID, module callback, and context to the `__cdecl` module-enumeration export and returns one.
- `0x005c0ac0`, `0x005c0b40`, `0x005c0ca0`, `0x005c0fa0`: generated Dinkumware tree allocation/insertion/rebalance/unique-insert/destruction, not authored methods. Best IDA names remain descriptive compiler-helper names; final source is `std::set` operations/destruction.
- `0x005c0eb0`: compiler EH cleanup for `Singleton<VirusChecker>` publication. Existing name/comment are retained.
- `0x005c0ec0`: compiler scalar deleting destructor generated from virtual `~VirusChecker()`. Existing name/type/comment are retained; no source wrapper is emitted.
- String type: exact historical alias is stripped. The best complete source is a private `ModulePathString` alias over the known pointer-backed ANSI StringBase specialization. Rejected `class String; std::set<String>` because a by-value set element needs a complete type and the current project emits no `String` alias.
- Comparator: default `std::set<String>` was plausible in old docs, but the generated header in the dated 2026-08-05 report-time readback had no `operator<` contract. A private stateless `ModulePathLess` calling `strcmp` is a complete, period-plausible representation of the exact observed ordering and preserves the two-word container footprint through empty-comparator optimization.
- Lowercasing: UID0004FM and live `AnsiStringBase_ToLowerCopy` prove a return-by-value `ToLower() const` specialization. `ModulePathString(path).ToLower()` best explains constructor, copy, detach, `_strlwr_s(length+1)`, and temporary destruction. The sole out-of-line body in the SoundManager consumer TU plus the inlined VirusChecker copy make header-visible `StringBase.h`/included-`.inl` placement the highest-probability original source shape; a generated `StringBase.cpp` body is an accommodation, not source-placement evidence.
- Vendor ABI: all four dynamically loaded exports are caller-cleaned in the binary and therefore `__cdecl`; callback returns use `retn 0x10` and `retn 0x14`, therefore `__stdcall`. Official-domain and exact-symbol web searches on 2026-08-02 found no authoritative legacy SDK declaration. Binary cleanup is decisive for calling convention; descriptive unsigned-long names are used for unused 32-bit callback slots.
- Open source-placement question is resolved to the existing `NexusTK/security/VirusChecker.cpp`/`.h` root. Vendor DLL implementation, Application, StringBase, and generic STL source are dependencies or rejected owners.

- Negative evidence: zero xrefs to `0x005c0940`, no VA/RVA/raw-offset pointer patterns for `0x005c0940`, `0x005c0eb0`, or `0x005c0fa0`, no official legacy callback prototypes, and no external activation route.
- The evidence supports source behavior and ABI more strongly than original spelling. Scores remain below final-audit levels for that reason, not because any required source decision was deferred.

## Evidence Checked

- MCP tools used during the historical bounded research pass: `server_health`, `analyze_function`, `disasm`, `xrefs_to`, `find_bytes`, `get_bytes`, `get_comments`, `stack_frame`, `entity_query`, `list_globals`, `inspect_items`, `get_global_value`, and `type_inspect`.
- Functions checked: all nine target starts plus scanner `0x005c07b0`, ordinary destructor `0x005c04e0`, StringBase dependencies `0x00582730`, `0x005829f0`, `0x00583050`, `0x00583450`, `0x00582900`, `0x00582b30`, `0x00584540`, shared tree helpers `0x005796d0`, `0x005796f0`, `0x00579760`, rotations `0x0043ed70`/`0x0043f000`, length-error helper `0x005c5eac`, and deallocator `0x005c7526`.
- Bytes checked: every internal alignment gap, predecessor/successor padding, the 28-byte zero-filled global cluster, and PE section/raw-offset mapping.
- Pointer-pattern searches checked `0x005c0940`, `0x005c0eb0`, and `0x005c0fa0` as VA, RVA, and raw-offset little-endian patterns. All nine searches returned zero.
- Documentation checked: target, class, file, aggregate, V3 load/scan, dynamic globals, process-tree layout, vtable, client anticheat, StringBase template/file/ToLower child, dated generated VirusChecker/StringBase outputs, research tracker, and exact matching executed reports.
- No authoritative AhnLab legacy SDK declaration was found. This limits only the exact historical spelling of unused vendor callback parameters; the binary ABI and source-facing best names are resolved.
- Prior report-embedded package and hostile-suite outputs are rejected as current evidence and no longer support any claim.
## Claim And Incorporation Ledger

Every ledger row names one non-collapsed Actor / authority. Its Claim ID, Target UID, normalized Actor / authority value, Action, and controlled Verification-state token must equal the corresponding Section 33 checklist row exactly. Normalization removes only the checklist's fixed `Target UID:` and `Actor / authority:` prefixes and the proof suffix beginning with the first `;` after the controlled state token; the normalized Target UID value itself must remain exact. The required set is exactly C1NW-001..117 in order with 61 checked and 56 unchecked rows.

C1NW-046..076 are the 31 structured IDA recommendations in Section 21: 16 actionable and 15 deterministic NO_CHANGE rows. The seven function-name actions use exact `rename.batch.func` items `{addr,name}` under `pure:true`, `stop_on_error:true`, and `allow_overwrite:false`; three stored data-name actions retain their distinct `{old,new}` items, while three unnamed slots use separate address-based `make_data {addr,type,name,delete_existing:false}` actions. C1NW-101 remains an unchecked Primary supervisor Gate2B process claim. The supervisor performs fresh runtime attestation, collision checks, backup, itemized MCP/IDA action, independent readback, save/rollback, process handling, and operational verification directly outside this report; no report-embedded program supplies proof.

## Ranked Ownership Analysis

### 1. VirusChecker class UID0000FW

- Evidence for: both callbacks receive/use `VirusChecker *` context; member uses object +4 set; destructor paths use the same set; V3 scanner passes the callbacks; all bodies are source consequences of private class state.
- Evidence limitation: bounded caller/xref, VA/RVA/raw-pointer, singleton-global-consumer, constructor/load/scan/helper-route, and matching report/document checks recovered no external activation route. This is a bounded negative result, not proof that no route can exist and not evidence against the class's direct semantic ownership.
- Decision: direct semantic owner and emitter. Route absence caps runtime confidence only.

### 2. VirusChecker file UID0000P5

- Evidence for: correct final source root and owner of module-static vendor pointers/handles.
- Evidence against: too broad to replace the class as direct semantic owner for methods/private state.
- Decision: final source root through class emitter, not direct target owner.

### 3. StringBase, STL, or AhnLab vendor source

- Evidence for: helper bodies use StringBase and Dinkumware tree mechanics; callbacks match vendor APIs.
- Evidence against: dependencies do not own the VirusChecker context, private set, callback policy, or scanner behavior. Vendor DLLs are dynamically called, not statically embedded.
- Decision: rejected as owners; retain as dependencies/evidence.

### Proposed new file/grouping, if applicable

- No new source file or by-memory child is required. Add a sibling generated `NexusTK/security/VirusChecker.h` through the existing class H channel and preserve `VirusChecker.cpp` as the source root.

```cpp
void VirusChecker::InsertModulePath(const char *path)
{
    m_modulePaths.insert(ModulePathString(path).ToLower());
}

int __stdcall VirusChecker::V3ModuleEnumCallback(
    unsigned long processId,
    unsigned long moduleId,
    const char *modulePath,
    void *context)
{
    VirusChecker *checker = static_cast<VirusChecker *>(context);
    checker->InsertModulePath(modulePath);
    return 1;
}

int __stdcall VirusChecker::V3ProcessEnumCallback(
    unsigned long processId,
    unsigned long unused1,
    unsigned long unused2,
    unsigned long unused3,
    void *context)
{
    s_pfnV3PCtrlEnumModules(processId, V3ModuleEnumCallback, context);
    return 1;
}
```

- Class H replacement:

```cpp
#ifndef NEXUSTK_SECURITY_VIRUSCHECKER_H
#define NEXUSTK_SECURITY_VIRUSCHECKER_H

#include <set>
#include <string.h>
#include "../util/Singleton.h"
#include "../util/StringBase.h"

class VirusChecker : public Singleton<VirusChecker>
{
public:
    VirusChecker();
    virtual ~VirusChecker();

    bool LoadV3Scanner();
    void ScanLoadedProcessModules();

private:
    typedef mystr::StringBase<char, mystr::mychar_traits<char> > ModulePathString;

    struct ModulePathLess
    {
        bool operator()(const ModulePathString& left,
                        const ModulePathString& right) const
        {
            return strcmp(left.c_str(), right.c_str()) < 0;
        }
    };

    typedef std::set<ModulePathString, ModulePathLess> ModulePathSet;

    static int __stdcall V3ModuleEnumCallback(
        unsigned long processId,
        unsigned long moduleId,
        const char *modulePath,
        void *context);
    static int __stdcall V3ProcessEnumCallback(
        unsigned long processId,
        unsigned long unused1,
        unsigned long unused2,
        unsigned long unused3,
        void *context);

    void InsertModulePath(const char *path);

    ModulePathSet m_modulePaths;
};

extern VirusChecker *g_pVirusChecker;

#endif
```

```cpp
namespace mystr {

template <>
inline StringBase<char, mychar_traits<char> >
StringBase<char, mychar_traits<char> >::ToLower() const
```
